*    Natural white LED portable work light provides accurate color visibility, long life span, and stays cool to the touch 
*    Three brightness settings of DEWALT work light are designed to customize work space illumination 
*    Telescoping pole of the rechargeable work light extends light head to 7 feet to reduce shadows and glare 
*    Latch mechanism on handle to easily extend and collapse legs 
*    Tough shroud protects light head and withstands jobsite conditions 
*    Over 11 hours runtime* 
*    IP-55 rating for weather and dust resistance

## Product Description

The DEWALT 20V MAX* Cordless Tripod LED Work Light illuminates your workspace in the toughest jobsite conditions. It's built for incredible durability in use, storage, or transport. Eliminate the hazardous heat of halogen. Cutting edge LED technology provides extended runtime and natural white light. Variable brightness settings up to 3000 lumens let you customize illumination for your work area. Compact design with carry handle provides easy portability, and it sets up in seconds. Rotating, pivoting head of LED rechargeable work lights directs light where you need it. This package includes the light only.

[How customer reviews and ratings work](javascript:void(0))

Customer Reviews, including Product Star Ratings help customers to learn more about the product and decide whether it is the right product for them.

To calculate the overall star rating and percentage breakdown by star, we don’t use a simple average. Instead, our system considers things like how recent a review is and if the reviewer bought the item on Amazon. It also analyzed reviews to verify trustworthiness.

[Learn more how customers reviews work on Amazon](https://www.amazon.com/gp/help/customer/display.html/ref=cm_cr_dp_d_omni_lm_btn?nodeId=G8UYX7LALQC8V9KA)

* * *

### Customers say

Customers find this work light to be one of the best on the market, praising its brightness that lights up 12x12 rooms and its well-built construction. They appreciate its portability, folding down into a neat cylinder for storage, and its ease of use, with one customer noting it can be set up and broken down in seconds. Customers like its adjustability, with one mentioning it telescopes up to 8 feet with an adjustable head. Battery life and durability receive mixed reviews - while some say it lasts over 3 hours on high, others find it goes through batteries quickly and report issues with the plastic leg hinge breaking.

_5 out of 5 stars_

##### The only thing missing is a shoulder strap

Earlier this year I purchased a cheap battery powered LED light with a 1/4" tripod mount and a cheap camera tripod and I figured all my work lighting needs were done. But it's a major pain transporting it around, sometimes there was no place for the tripod legs to go where I needed to work, the LED light did not pivot down, the battery on the light didn't last and I was suddenly in the dark, and many times I wished the light was brighter. So I finally broke down and purchased this when it went on sale. All the good things everyone has said about this light are true, so I won't go into that. For a longevity test I used the new Jialitt 12 Ah battery and it ran for 6.5 hours on a single charge on the brightest setting. On the lowest setting I'm thinking it will run for a whole 24 hours, but I'm not putting my brand new battery through another full discharge cycle to find out for certain. I've found that having this extended next to my workbench pivoted down provides way more light than what the two 100 Watt bulbs produce in the overhead fixture above the workbench. So I'm even using it at home when I'm soldering circuit boards or working on scooters. I don't even have to extend the tripod legs- it stands there fine on it's own, but a little string to keep it from leaning too much helps. ;) One thing I noticed is that the handle seems to be balanced for when no battery is installed. So apparently that's how it's designed to be carried around. Because if you add the battery, it's no longer balanced and the rear will tend to drop to a lower position which makes it a little awkward to carry long distances. I really wish this came with a shoulder strap, but alas- I don't even see support clips needed for adding one. I'm sure there are good reasons for carrying the battery separately, but its a little inconvenient.

Full content visible, double tap to read brief content. 12/10/19 - I got this light last week and have used it every day since. I work on houses after my day job so this time of year I work in the dark. it is more than enough to light up the whole room just by pointing it at the ceiling. I already kicked it over a few times moving wood around it does not even have a scratch on it. It has 3 light setting so you don't chew through your battery as fast, don't let the battery life fool you, if you run this thing on on the highest setting using a 2mah battery you are going to need to change it out every hour or so. i have a bunch laying around so it does not bother me much.

PROS -

easy to set up,

easy to move around,

multiple light settings,

goes high enough to not shine you in the face,

holds a 60V batter for longer life.

CONS -

on high settings you will chew through your batteries,

if there is a battery in the bottom and you go to move it when it is closed up, it it is unbalanced forcing you to use the upper hand hold ( see next comment to why this is important )

the hand holds at the top are not very deep and the back digs into your skin (if you are not wearing gloves it won't take long to make you bleed)

would be nice to have a plug in on the bottom to charge a battery in case power is close ( this way you don't have to change out a battery every hour if you are running it on full power). there is more than enough room at the bottom to have a plug in, no excuse for not having one.

head does not swivel, so if you need to move the head you have to pick up the whole stand and move it. problem with that is you may not have enough footing to do so (sometimes you are up in the rafters where footing is limited). even a 30 or 40 degree movement is enough not to bind the cord but still move the head enough to get you where you need it.

OVERALL - would recommend for sure, has more than enough light, easy to move and set up, aims down so if you work on cars like i do it will shine down enough to see the engine, multiple light setting so you if you don't want to blind pilots flying overhead with the beam you can turn it down       [Read more Read less](javascript:void(0))       22 people found this helpful Helpful  Sending feedback...Thank you for your feedback. Full content visible, double tap to read brief content. As the photos show below, the light illuminates a pretty large area. It does telescope up 7' and the directional head allows the light to be easily aimed. The way the unit folds up for transportation it is relatively easy to carry up a ladder--even two story buildings. We are a commercial roofing company that offers 24/7 repairs. This light is indispensable. With two of these lights, one on each side of our roof repair area, a huge area is illuminated well enough to do detailed repair jobs.

Another feature I love is the warning it gives prior to needing a new battery. It intermittently flashes for about 15 minutes before a battery is drained.

The only thing it lacks is the ability to plug in a power cord. That would make it much more convenient than changing out batteries.

I HIGHLY recommend this light.

P.S. We also have a DeWalt DCL070 light that looks like a fire hydrant. I really dislike this light because it is so heavy we can not carry it up a ladder for roof repairs. Also, the DCL070 does not telescope up like the DCL079B.       [Read more Read less](javascript:void(0))

The only thing it lacks is the ability to plug in a power cord. That would make it much more convenient than changing out batteries.

I HIGHLY recommend this light.

P.S. We also have a DeWalt DCL070 light that looks like a fire hydrant. I really dislike this light because it is so heavy we can not carry it up a ladder for roof repairs. Also, the DCL070 does not telescope up like the DCL079B.

# Product Summary: DEWALT 20V MAX LED Work Light with Stand, Tripod Light, 3000 Lumens of Brightness with 3 Modes, Tool Only (DCL079B)

From DEWALT

4.8 out of 5 stars, 2,446 ratings

[Customer reviews](https://www.amazon.com/dp/B077ZCTBFY?ref=dp_product_quick_view#customer-reviews_feature_div)

## Price

One-time purchase: $249.00

[Purchasing options](https://www.amazon.com/dp/B077ZCTBFY?ref=dp_product_quick_view#buybox)

[Other sellers](https://www.amazon.com/dp/B077ZCTBFY?ref=dp_product_quick_view#dynamic-aod-ingress-box)

[Other sellers](https://www.amazon.com/dp/B077ZCTBFY?ref=dp_product_quick_view#unqualifiedBuyBox)

## About this Item

*   Natural white LED portable work light provides accurate color visibility, long life span, and stays cool to the touch
*   Three brightness settings of DEWALT work light are designed to customize work space illumination
*   Telescoping pole of the rechargeable work light extends light head to 7 feet to reduce shadows and glare
*   Latch mechanism on handle to easily extend and collapse legs
*   Tough shroud protects light head and withstands jobsite conditions
*   Over 11 hours runtime*
*   IP-55 rating for weather and dust resistance

## Product Description

 The DEWALT 20V MAX* Cordless Tripod LED Work Light illuminates your workspace in the toughest jobsite conditions. It's built for incredible durability in use, storage, or transport. Eliminate the hazardous heat of halogen. Cutting edge LED technology provides extended runtime and natural white light. Variable brightness settings up to 3000 lumens let you customize illumination for your work area. Compact design with carry handle provides easy portability, and it sets up in seconds. Rotating, pivoting head of LED rechargeable work lights directs light where you need it. This package includes the light only.
